# 4.0.0 tool and input migration tables

Generated from the 3.x archive at manifest/history/contracts-v3/baseline, the shipped V4 baselines at manifest/contracts/v4/baseline, and the reviewed current-to-V4 map in reference/tool-catalog/v3-v4-renames.json.

Each release table lists every archived 3.x name and its V4 destination, including unchanged names. The generator verifies every archived name's release availability, every mapping target against that release's V4 baseline, and the complete V4 inventory after additions. Historical names below are not registered as aliases.

### Release 14sp1

3.x catalog: **57 tools**; V4 baseline: **279 tools**. Unchanged names: 21; renamed names: 30; merged legacy entries: 0; V4-only tools: 228.

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

New tools and their required inputs

| V4-only tool | Required inputs in the shipped V4 baseline |
|---|---|
| `AnalyzeGlobalLibraryPackage` | `libraryPath`: `string` |
| `AnalyzeHmiTemplateReference` | `templateDirectory`: `string`, `referenceProjectPath`: `string`, `referenceGlobalLibraryPath`: `string` |
| `AnalyzePlcReferences` | `directory`: `string` |
| `AnalyzePlcSclSource` | none |
| `ApplyGenerationPlan` | `planId`: `string`, `expectedPlanHash`: `string`, `expectedProjectFile`: `string` |
| `ApplyToolBatch` | `previewToken`: `string` |
| `ArchiveSavedProject` | `archivePath`: `string` |
| `AttachDeviceNodeToSubnet` | `deviceItemPath`: `string`, `interfaceIndex`: `integer`, `subnetName`: `string` |
| `AuditEngineeringExports` | `directoryPath`: `string` |
| `BuildAndImportPlcArtifact` | `softwarePath`: `string`, `kind`: `string`, `spec`: `object` |

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (required) | `outputReleaseKey`: `string` (optional) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
| — | `limit`: `integer` (optional) |
| — | `offset`: `integer` (optional) |

3.x catalog: **488 tools**; V4 baseline: **511 tools**. Unchanged names: 304; renamed names: 176; merged legacy entries: 2; V4-only tools: 31.
